| [Opera Neon](https://www.operaneon.com/) | — | web | Opera's subscription agentic browser whose Neon Do agent opens, closes and acts across tabs inside the user's logged-in session, with task workspaces, prompt cards and MCP or CLI access for outside agents. | [✓ 2026‑10‑18](https://www.operaneon.com/) |

| [Twin](https://twin.so) | — | web | No-code platform that turns plain-language goals into scheduled business agents, calling APIs where they exist and falling back to a Web Agent that clicks, types and fills forms in an isolated cloud browser. | [✓ 2026‑10‑20](https://twin.so) |

| 2026‑01‑22 | [Computer Environments Elicit General Agentic Intelligence in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16206) | unspecified | LLM-in-Sandbox gives agents a minimal computer environment for resource access, file management and code execution; LLM-in-Sandbox-RL trains models to use it. |
| 2026‑01‑14 | [GUI-Eyes: Tool-Augmented Perception for Visual Grounding in GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.09770) | unspecified | GUI-Eyes trains GUI grounders to decide when and how to use visual tools such as crops and zooms. | 2026‑01‑12 | [OS-Symphony: A Holistic Framework for Robust and Generalist Computer-Using Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07779) | desktop, web | OS-Symphony coordinates milestone-based reflection and memory with tools that build visually aligned tutorials through browser interaction. | 2025‑12‑29 | [Nested Browser-Use Learning for Agentic Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23647) | web | NestBrowse organizes browser actions in a nested framework that separates interaction control from page exploration. | 2025‑12‑26 | [MAI-UI Technical Report: Real-World Centric Foundation GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22047) | android | MAI-UI combines GUI navigation, native user interaction, and MCP tool calls in a family of policies trained through generated data and online reinforcement learning. | 2025‑12‑22 | [MobileWorld: Benchmarking Autonomous Mobile Agents in Agent-User Interactive and MCP-Augmented Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.19432) | android | Introduces long mobile workflows that combine agent-user interaction, cross-application tasks, and MCP-based tool access. |
| 2025‑12‑17 | [Step-GUI Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15431) | android, desktop | Step-GUI uses calibrated trajectory rewards to turn generated GUI interaction data into training signals for compact agent models. | 2025‑12‑07 | [Permission Manifests for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02371) | web | Proposes agent-permissions.json, a website manifest for declaring which interactions web agents may perform, with optional references to APIs. |

| 2025‑11‑22 | [Building Browser Agents: Architecture, Security, and Practical Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.19477) | web | Reports architectural and security lessons from building a browser agent, including hybrid accessibility-tree and visual context, browser tooling and programmatic constraints. A WebGames evaluation accompanies the discussion of reliability and specialization. |
| 2025‑10‑28 | [OSWorld-MCP: Benchmarking MCP Tool Invocation In Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24563) | desktop | OSWorld-MCP evaluates how agents choose between GUI operations and MCP tool calls in computer tasks. | 2025‑10‑20 | [UltraCUA: A Foundation Model for Computer Use Agents with Hybrid Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.17790) | desktop, windows | UltraCUA trains a shared policy for primitive GUI actions and high-level tool calls using synthesized tasks and hybrid trajectories. | 2025‑10‑06 | [3Dify: a Framework for Procedural 3D-CG Generation Assisted by LLMs Using MCP and RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04536) | unspecified | Introduces 3Dify, a natural-language 3D graphics framework that operates creation tools through MCP and uses a computer-using agent for GUI operations when MCP is unavailable. |

| 2025‑10‑05 | [\textsc{GUI-Spotlight}: Adaptive Iterative Focus Refinement for Enhanced GUI Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04039) | unspecified | GUI-Spotlight trains a grounding model to reason over images and call tools that iteratively narrow its visual focus. | 2025‑10‑01 | [WALT: Web Agents that Learn Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01524) | web | WALT explores website functionality and converts demonstrated interactions into validated, reusable tools combining DOM actions, URL navigation, and agentic fallbacks. | 2025‑09‑30 | [Ferret-UI Lite: Lessons from Building Small On-Device GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26539) | android, web, desktop, cross-platform | Ferret-UI Lite develops a 3B GUI agent using mixed real and synthetic data, reasoning with visual tools at inference, and reinforcement learning. | 2025‑09‑26 | [Secure and Efficient Access Control for Computer-Use Agents via Context Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22256) | unspecified | Introduces CSAgent, which combines static access policies with user intent and context and enforces them through an operating-system service. | 2025‑09‑14 | [Agentic Lybic: Multi-Agent Execution System with Tiered Reasoning and Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.11067) | desktop | Introduces Agentic Lybic, a desktop automation system that routes tasks among GUI, code, and decision-support workers using a finite-state machine and evaluator. |

| 2025‑07‑30 | [Magentic-UI: Towards Human-in-the-loop Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.22358) | web | Magentic-UI combines browser interaction, code execution, file operations, and MCP tools in an interface for human-agent collaboration. | 2023‑10‑24 | [WebWISE: Web Interface Control and Sequential Exploration with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.16042) | web | WebWISE filters DOM elements into observations and generates short programs for click, scroll, and text-entry steps. | 2023‑10‑16 | [OpenAgents: An Open Platform for Language Agents in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.10634) | web | OpenAgents hosts three language agents, for data analysis, API plugins and autonomous web browsing, behind a web interface for non-expert users, with local deployment for developers. |
| 2023‑05‑30 | [SheetCopilot: Bringing Software Productivity to the Next Level through Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.19308) | desktop, windows | Introduces SheetCopilot, an agent that steers spreadsheet software via atomic virtual APIs, along with a 221-task spreadsheet manipulation benchmark. |
